## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你查查邮件和日程。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's clear.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 이 컴퓨터에서 특정 저장소의 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시거나, 메일·일정처럼 연결된 계정에서 필요한 정보를 찾아 달라고 하시면 바로 도와드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 불편 없으셨으면 좋겠어요. 내일 아침에 봐요. 필요한 거 생기면 그때 말해 주세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確認しながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信、UDPは「確認や順番の管理をせず、速さを優先して送りっぱなしにする」通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, o para traer los cambios de la rama principal a tu rama de trabajo sin ensuciar el historial. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya compartiste con otros, porque rebase reescribe commits y eso rompe el trabajo de quien ya los tiene.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输：先握手建立连接，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 则是无连接、不保证可靠，只管尽快发出去，换来低延迟和低开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpublished branch to bring it up to date and keep history linear before you share it. Merge when others already have the branch, or when you want the record of how the work actually happened.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名或一段路径，Bun 会按它匹配要跑的文件。如果只想跑某个用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it intends to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to finish or roll back those changes, so no committed data is lost.
```

const CATS = ['交通','住宿','餐饮','其他'];
let items = [], cat = '交通', editing = -1;

const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt = n => '¥ … = b.dataset.cat; renderCats();

$('list').onclick = e => {
  const r = e.target.closest('.k-row'); if (!r) return;
  editing = +r.dataset.idx;
  const it = items[editing];
  $('name').value = it.name; $('amt').value = it.amt; $('date').value = it.date || '';
  cat = it.cat; renderCats();
  $('addtitle').textContent = '改这一笔';
  $('save').textContent = '保存修改';
  $('del').style.display = '';
  $('add').open = true;
};

$('save').onclick = async () => {
  const name = $('name').value.trim();
  const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!name || !(amt > 0)) { ($('name').value ? $('amt') : $('name')).focus(); return; }
  const item = { name, amt, cat, date: $('date').value.trim() };
  if (editing >= 0) {
    item.from = items[editing].from;
    items[editing] = item;
  } else {
    items.push(item);
  }
  clearForm();
  $('add').open = false;
  await persist();
};

$('del').onclick = async () => {
  if (editing < 0) return;
  items.splice(editing, 1);
  clearForm();
  $('add').open = false;
  await persist();
};

async function load(){
  const v = await ducter.get('expenses');
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}

renderCats();
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"name":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","amt":662,"cat":"交通","date":"11/20","from":"你的预订"},{"name":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amt":1036,"cat":"住宿","date":"11/20–21","from":"你的预订"}]}

### app-renew-zh

```
(earlier) user: 帮我订明天去北京的高铁，再订两晚酒店，离 agibar 近一点
(earlier) partner: 订好了：11/20 G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南，¥662；亚朵酒店（望京）11/20 和 11/21 两晚，¥1,036。
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 把「出差花销」换成新样子，能填的先帮我填好
  [partner message] 做好了，高铁 ¥662 和亚朵两晚 ¥1,036 已经填进去，合计 ¥1,698；以后记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2334 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这趟出差合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" id="c1" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" id="c2" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat='交通';
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const money=n=>'¥'+Number(n||0).toLocaleString('en-US');
function pick(c){cat=c;$("c1").setAttribute('aria-pressed',c==='交通');$("c2").setAttribute('aria-pressed',c==='住宿')}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  const by=c=>xs.filter(x=>x.cat===c).reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("total").innerHTML=xs.length?money(total):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $("sub").innerHTML=xs.length?'<span>交通 '+money(by('交通'))+'</span><span>住宿 '+money(by('住宿'))+'</span>':'<span>记了花销就在这里合计</span>';
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+' 笔,合计 '+money(total):'还没记');
  $("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.note||x.trip)+'<div class="k-row-sub"><span>'+esc(x.cat||'')+'</span><span>'+esc(x.day||'')+'</span></div></div><div class="k-row-value">'+money(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join(''):'<p class="k-empty">还没有花销，记一笔就会列在这里</p>';
}
$("c1").onclick=()=>pick('交通');
$("c2").onclick=()=>pick('住宿');
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim()||'出差',day:$("day").value.trim(),cat,amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value='';
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

- 用假时钟，不要依赖真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过；在 ~/dev/foo 的同一分支上提交；报告里附上测试输出，并连续运行多次确认稳定。

1. 在 Safari 下验证登录页的重定向修复是否正常（登录后 next 参数回跳，不再回到 /login 自己）。
2. 补一个测试，覆盖 Safari 下的这个场景（或把现有测试扩展到 Safari 环境），与已有的 3 个测试一起跑通。
3. 在自己的分支上提交，并报告：Safari 验证的结果、新测试的名字和运行结果。

不做的：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。

地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo。
